TL;DR: 本文提出P-JEPA，一种骨干网络无关的长视频表示学习方法，通过将问题简化为密集的帧对齐动作空间并预测池化掩码潜向量，实现对30分钟以上程序性视频的有效理解，在EgoExo4D等数据集上达到SOTA性能，参数比LLM方法少一个数量级且支持实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型由于自注意力的二次复杂度，无法支持程序性视频中具有长程依赖的动作（如视觉相似但功能不同的开关炉灶），需要一种能高效处理长视频（30分钟以上）的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出Procedural Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (P-JEPA)，是一种骨干网络无关的方法。通过将问题简化为密集的帧对齐动作空间，预测池化掩码潜向量，从而支持超过30分钟的长视频输入。

Result: 在EgoExo4D、EgoProceL和Assembly101数据集上，使用VJEPA2.1、TSM、I3D特征，在线性分离性、流式推理和时间动作分割上持续提升。在EgoExo4D细粒度动作分类上达到SOTA，参数比LLM方法少一个数量级，支持实时运行。

Conclusion: P-JEPA能够有效学习长程序性视频的表示，解决了现有视频模型因自注意力二次复杂度而无法处理长程依赖的问题，在多个基准上取得优异性能，且计算效率高。

Abstract: The increasing maturity of embodied AI platforms has driven a growing interest in procedural video representation learning to support intelligent assistance systems for complex, multi-step tasks. Leveraging large-scale latent predictive training, video foundation models capture video dynamics, enabling downstream tasks such as activity understanding, spatiotemporal localization, and predictive control. However, procedural videos include actions with long-range dependencies that these models do not support, due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Distinct actions, for example, may be visually similar despite appearing at different points in the procedure, such as turning the stove on versus off. Here, we propose a backbone-agnostic approach that learns long-duration video representations by reducing the problem to a dense, frame-aligned action space and predicting pooled masked latent vectors. This approach allows our Procedural Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (P-JEPA) to ingest videos over 30 minutes long, enabling effective long-form understanding of procedural steps. We evaluate P-JEPA using features extracted with VJEPA2.1, TSM, and I3D over the EgoExo4D, EgoProceL, and Assembly101 datasets, finding that it consistently improves linear separability, streaming inference, and temporal action segmentation performance, achieving state-of-the-art results on EgoExo4D fine-grained action classification while using an order of magnitude fewer parameters than LLM-based methods and running in real time.

</details>


### [251] [Safe Few-Step Generation via Velocity Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23267)
*Yujin Choi,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对流匹配文本到图像生成模型采样步数少、传统安全方法不适配的问题，提出无训练的安全方法VESFlow/VESFlow+，通过编辑速度场实现概念去除，在4步MeanFlow模型上将攻击成功率降至6.3%-6.8%，同时保持良性提示的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配文本到图像生成模型（如MeanFlow）在少步采样下难以适配现有的安全方法（如迭代轨迹矫正或CLIP嵌入操控），亟需一种针对流匹配范式、无需额外训练且能在极少采样步数下有效去除不安全内容的安全方法。

Method: 利用流匹配模型学习边际速度的特性，通过安全条件后验直接编辑速度场，在不改变条件提示的前提下将轨迹导向安全输出。基于VESFlow对良性提示输出不变的特点，引入风险评分过滤跳过速度编辑以降低计算开销，并进一步提出VESFlow+，不仅向安全方向编辑速度，还远离不安全方向。

Result: 在4步MeanFlow模型上，VESFlow+将Ring-A-Bell和MMA-Diffusion数据集的NudeNet攻击成功率分别降至6.3%和6.8%，同时保持良性提示的生成保真度。

Conclusion: VESFlow系列方法有效解决了流匹配T2I模型在极少采样步数下的安全概念去除问题，为流匹配生成范式的安全部署提供了可行的无训练方案。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently emerged as a strong paradigm for state-of-the-art text-to-image (T2I) generation, enabling high-quality generation with a small number of sampling steps. As these models are increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring safe and non-sensitive content generation has become a critical requirement. However, adapting safety and concept removal methods to this new generation framework remains an open challenge. Specifically, prior methods largely rely on iterative trajectory steering across a number of denoising steps or on CLIP-centric prompt embedding manipulation. These design assumptions pose fundamental bottlenecks for safety in flow matching-based T2I generation, where limited sampling steps constrain iterative correction and modern context-aware text encoders diminish the effectiveness of embedding-level interventions. In this paper, we propose VESFlow, a training-free safety method tailored to flow matching with extremely few sampling steps. Leveraging the fact that flow matching models learn the marginal velocity, we directly edit the velocity field via a safe-conditional posterior. VESFlow steers the trajectory toward safe outputs while leaving the conditioning prompt unchanged. Building on the observation that VESFlow leaves outputs unchanged under benign prompts, we further introduce a risk score-based filtering that bypasses velocity editing to reduce computational cost while preserving benign prompt generation. Based on this filtering, we propose VESFlow+, a stronger variant of VESFlow that not only edits the velocity toward the safe direction, but also pushes it away from the unsafe direction. Experimental results show that VESFlow+ removes the target concept, reducing the attack success rate by NudeNet to 6.3% on Ring-A-Bell and 6.8% on MMA-Diffusion on the 4-step MeanFlow model, while preserving fidelity on benign prompts.

</details>


### [252] [BoxCtrl: 3D-Aware Visual Prompting for Geometric Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23270)
*Feifei Wang,Shiyuan Yang,Xiaoyu Li,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoxCtrl提出了一种3D感知的视觉提示框架，通过在2D图像上投影带有不同颜色编码的RGB 3D边界框作为视觉提示，结合SFT和强化学习两阶段训练，实现了精确的3D几何编辑（平移、旋转、缩放等），在多项任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的编辑和多模态大模型虽能完成多种图像编辑任务，但在3D空间中的精确几何编辑（如平移、缩放、旋转）仍面临重大挑战，缺乏有效的几何控制手段。

Method: 提出BoxCtrl框架，核心是使用三面涂有不同RGB颜色的3D边界框投影到2D图像作为视觉提示，解耦几何控制与外观控制。采用两阶段训练：先在大规模合成数据集上进行监督微调（SFT），再在未配对真实数据上使用强化学习（RL）优化，奖励函数同时评估几何精度和视觉保真度。

Result: 在平移、旋转、缩放及复合编辑任务上均达到SOTA性能，在保持照片级真实感的同时显著提升了几何编辑精度。

Conclusion: 精心设计的彩色3D边界框能有效编码几何信息，SFT+RL的训练策略成功弥合了合成数据与真实数据之间的域差距，为3D感知的几何图像编辑提供了一种高效且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: As instruction-based editing models and multimodal large language models advance, diverse image editing tasks have become feasible. However, achieving precise and consistent geometric image editing, such as translating, scaling, and rotating in 3D space, remains a major challenge. In this work, we introduce BoxCtrl, a 3D-aware visual prompting framework. Unlike text-only or coarse 2D-guided approaches, our method introduces informative RGB 3D bounding boxes projected onto 2D images as visual prompts. The three orthogonal faces of each box are painted with distinct RGB colors, simultaneously encoding position, size, and orientation to provide a compact, intuitive in-context visual example. The key to BoxCtrl's success lies in these well-designed bounding boxes, which decouple geometric control from appearance control. This enables the model to learn consistent correspondences between faces of the same color in the latent space, leading to a precise understanding of geometric intentions and accurate editing results. We introduce a two-stage training paradigm: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) followed by Reinforcement Learning (RL). To address paired data scarcity, we construct a large-scale synthetic dataset for SFT, equipping the model with fundamental editing capabilities. To bridge the synthetic-to-real domain gap, we incorporate an online RL stage leveraging unpaired real-world data. Guided by a reward function evaluating geometric accuracy and visual fidelity, our SFT-RL strategy significantly enhances geometric precision while maintaining photorealistic quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BoxCtrl achieves state-of-the-art performance across translation, rotation, scaling, and composite editing tasks.

</details>


### [253] [Transfer learning-based method for automated ewaste recycling in smart cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23286)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,Paul Szabo-Müller,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用迁移学习（基于AlexNet预训练模型）对智能手机进行智能分类，以支持自动化电子废弃物回收。在包含6个品牌12种类别的小型数据集上，通过调整学习率、选择优化器和数据增强，使用带动量的随机梯度下降优化器和3e-4学习率达到了约98%的分类准确率。研究结果表明，人工智能驱动的自动化分类可显著降低电子废弃物分拣的错误率，助力循环智慧城市建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日益增长的电子废弃物（e-waste）流需要高效的自动化回收方案，传统方法难以在短时间内准确分类大量废弃物。将人工智能与循环经济结合，能够推动智能电子废弃物回收，从而实现循环智慧城市。

Method: 采用迁移学习技术，对预训练的AlexNet模型进行微调（finetuning），在包含6个智能手机品牌、12个类别的小型数据集上进行分类。通过调整学习率、选择最优优化器（带动量的随机梯度下降SGD with Momentum）以及数据增强（augmentation）来防止过拟合，优化模型性能。

Result: 使用带动量的随机梯度下降优化器，学习率设置为3e-4，模型准确率达到约98%，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于迁移学习的AI智能分类方法能有效降低电子废弃物分拣的错误率，是应对当前电子废弃物回收挑战的最佳方案之一，为自动化电子废弃物回收和循环智慧城市发展提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Sorting a huge stream of waste accurately within a short period can be done with the support of digitalization, particularly Artificial Intelligence, instead of traditional methods. The overlap of Artificial Intelligence and Circular Economy can flourish many services in the environmental technology domain, in particular smart ewaste recycling, resulting in enabling circular smart cities. We analyse the growing need for automated ewaste recycling as an essential requirement to cope with the fast growing ewaste stream and we shed the light on the impact of Artificial Intelligence in supporting the recycling process through smart classification of devices, where the smartphone is our case study. Our study applies transfer learning as a special technique of Artificial Intelligence by finetuning the output layers of AlexNet as a pretrained model and perform the implementation on a small size dataset that contains 12 classes from 6 smartphone brands. We evaluate the performance of our model by tuning the learning rate, choosing the best optimizer, and augmenting the original dataset to avoid overfitting. We found that the optimizer of Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum and 3e-4 as a learning rate brings almost 98% model accuracy with generalization. Our study supports automated ewaste recycling in decreasing the error rate of ewaste sorting and investigates the advantages of applying transfer learning as the best scenario to overcome the rising challenges.

</details>


### [254] [Flow6D: Discrete-to-Continuous Flow Matching for Efficient and Accurate Category-Level 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23293)
*Mingyu Mei,Li Zhang,Zibo Dai,Han Sun,Xinyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flow6D提出了一种层次化流匹配框架，采用“离散潜在空间定位-连续位姿回归”策略，先离散化旋转和平移参数为bins，通过离散流匹配锁定真实位姿附近的潜在空间，再用连续流匹配预测局部残差以优化位姿估计，在合成和真实数据集上达到SOTA且支持70 FPS实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级6D位姿估计方法直接在高维连续空间回归，面临两个关键挑战：噪声和局部最优导致精度有限，以及无限空间搜索低效阻碍实时性能。

Method: 提出Flow6D框架，包含两级策略：(1) 离散潜在空间定位——将旋转和平移参数离散化为bins，用离散流匹配模型锁定真实位姿附近的潜在空间，降低搜索复杂度；(2) 连续位姿回归——在潜在空间中采样，用连续流匹配模型预测局部位姿残差以优化估计并回归精确位姿。框架还可自然扩展到铰接物体。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上超越现有方法，达到70 FPS实时推理性能。

Conclusion: Flow6D通过层次化离散-连续流匹配有效解决了类别级6D位姿估计的精度与效率问题，并具备对铰接物体的泛化能力。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation is a key task in computer vision and embodied AI, widely used in robotic manipulation, augmented reality, etc. Existing methods directly regress in a high-dimensional continuous space, facing two key challenges in category-level pose estimation: limited accuracy due to noise and local optima, and inefficient search over an infinite space that hinders real-time performance. This paper proposes Flow6D, a hierarchical flow matching framework with a two-stage discrete latent space localization-continuous pose regression strategy. Rotation and translation parameters are first discretized into bins, with a discrete flow matching model locking the latent space around the true pose to reduce search complexity. Then, by sampling in the latent space, a continuous flow matching model predicts local pose residuals to optimize the estimate and regress to an accurate pose. The framework also naturally extends to articulated objects, outperforming state-of-the-art methods on synthetic and real datasets with real-time inference at 70 FPS. Project website: https://flow6d.github.io/.

</details>


### [255] [Ocean4D: Generative Underwater 4D Reconstruction via Medium-Aware Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23298)
*Yuqiang Huang,Yuxi Wang,Junyu Dong,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ocean4D生成式框架，通过4D几何一致条件构建（4D-GCC）和介质感知去噪模块，实现水下单目视频的4D重建，在动态和静态水下基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下4D重建方法沿用空气介质假设，未显式建模水下吸收和背向散射，且近静态假设对动态粒子干扰敏感，导致几何不稳定和跨视角不一致。

Method: 构建双组件框架：1) 4D-GCC生成4D几何一致条件，改善跨帧覆盖；2) 介质感知块在潜在扩散过程中隐式执行介质感知去噪，稳定吸收和散射下的水下外观。

Result: 在动态和静态水下基准上均达到最先进的重建性能。

Conclusion: Ocean4D有效解决了水下介质耦合问题，通过生成式方法实现了稳定、一致的4D重建。

Abstract: Underwater 4D reconstruction remains challenging due to the coupling between degraded light transport in participating media and dynamic water variations. Most existing Methods are developed under in-air assumptions and do not explicitly account for underwater absorption and backscatter. Additionally, near-static assumptions make these approaches sensitive to drifting particles and dynamic distractors , leading to unstable geometry and inconsistent cross-view results. To address these issues, we propose a generative framework for underwater 4D reconstruction, named Ocean4D, which is built on two complementary components. Specifically, 4D-GCC constructs 4D geometrically consistent conditioning with improved cross-frame coverage, while the Medium-Aware Block performs implicit medium-aware denoising in the latent diffusion process to stabilize underwater appearance under absorption and scattering. Given a monocular video and target cameras, our method generates videos along the target trajectories while preserving global structure and cross-view consistency. Extensive experiments on both dynamic and static underwater benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on underwater reconstruction.

</details>


### [256] [VideoAgent: All-in-One Framework for Video Understanding and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23327)
*Hengji Zhou,Lingxuan Huang,Jian Wang,Bing Zhou,Si Wu,Lianghao Xia,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoAgent是一个全能的智能体框架，通过自动镜头创建和多智能体编排（整合30多个专业编辑智能体）解决视频编辑中的长视频理解和多样化操作两大挑战，在VideoEdit基准测试中实现87-95%编排成功率并降低60%API成本，生成内容接近人类专业水平（评分仅低4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动视频编辑系统受限于短片段处理和领域特定任务，缺乏对多样化视频理解和编辑操作的处理能力，同时缺少长视频理解以实现连贯叙事创作。

Method: 提出VideoAgent框架，包含两大创新：1）自动视频镜头创建：镜头规划智能体生成连贯叙事段落，跨模态检索对齐视觉内容；2）多智能体编排框架：集成30多个专业编辑智能体，通过意图解析过滤相关工具，文本梯度图优化构建复杂编辑流程。

Result: 在新提出的VideoEdit基准和公共数据集上，VideoAgent优于多模态大模型和现有智能体系统，实现87-95%编排成功率，API成本降低60%。人类评估显示其在六个视频类别中生成的专业质量内容接近人类水平，评分仅比人工视频低4%。

Conclusion: VideoAgent有效解决了长视频理解和多样化编辑操作的挑战，是首个全能的自动化视频编辑框架，在性能和成本方面均优于现有方法，生成内容质量接近人类专业水平。

Abstract: Video editing has become essential in digital media creation, yet existing automated systems are restricted to short segment processing and domain-specific tasks. They face two critical limitations: i) inability to handle diverse video comprehension and editing operations, and ii) lack of long-video understanding for coherent narrative creation. We propose VideoAgent, an all-in-one agentic framework addressing these challenges through two key innovations. First, we develop automated video shot creation with shot planning agents for coherent narratives and cross-modal retrieval for aligned visual content. Second, we design a multi-agent orchestration framework integrating over thirty specialized editing agents. Intent parsing filters relevant tools while textual-gradient graph optimization assembles complex editing pipelines. Extensive experiments on our newly-proposed VideoEdit benchmark and public datasets demonstrate VideoAgent's superiority over existing multimodal LLMs and agentic systems. VideoAgent achieves 87-95% orchestration success rates while reducing API costs by 60%. Human evaluation across six video categories shows VideoAgent produces professional-quality content approaching human-level performance, with ratings only 4% below human-created videos. We release our code at https://github.com/HKUDS/VideoAgent.

</details>


### [257] [RT-DocLayout: Real-Time End-to-End Document Layout Analysis with Reading Order in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23344)
*Cheng Cui,Tingquan Gao,Xueqing Wang,Changda Zhou,Hongen Liu,Ting Sun,Yubo Zhang,Zelun Zhang,Jiaxuan Liu,Manhui Lin,Yue Zhang,Suyin Liang,Yiqing Xiang,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RT-DocLayout 是一个高效端到端文档布局分析框架，基于 RT-DETR 构建，仅用33M参数统一了分类、检测、分割和阅读顺序预测任务，在保持实时推理速度（132.1 FPS）的同时取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档布局分析方法存在碎片化多阶段流程或计算量大的生成式Transformer架构，导致误差传播和效率低下；此外，文档元素异质性、几何畸变和阅读顺序的复杂耦合仍是瓶颈。

Method: 以 RT-DETR 为基础，提出统一多任务公式，在单一查询解码器中同时进行分类、边界框回归、掩码生成和阅读顺序关系构建；通过多任务优化联合学习几何和结构表示。

Result: 公开基准上取得SOTA性能，实时推理速度达132.1 FPS；与下游OCR引擎结合后显著提升全文档重建质量。

Conclusion: RT-DocLayout 为文档智能系统提供了可扩展且实用的基础，验证了统一多任务端到端框架在文档布局分析中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Accurate document layout analysis remains a critical bottleneck for document parsing systems, due to the intricate coupling among heterogeneous document layout elements, geometric distortions (\eg, paper warping and bending, perspective variations), and reading order within diverse layout structures. Existing approaches typically rely on fragmented multi-stage pipelines or computationally heavy generative Transformer architectures, leading to error propagation and limited efficiency.
  In this paper, we present RT-DocLayout, a highly efficient end-to-end framework for document layout analysis, designed as a front-end for document parsing tasks. The proposed model unifies classification, detection, pixel-level segmentation, and reading order prediction for layout elements within a single 33M-parameter architecture. Built upon the RT-DETR, our key contribution is a unified multi-task formulation within a single query-based decoder that simultaneously classifies, regresses bounding box, generates masks, and constructs relationship to reason reading order.
  By jointly learning geometric and structural representations, RT-DocLayout introduces multi-task optimization that substantially improves robustness under real-world document distortions. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in document layout analysis while maintaining real-time inference speed(132.1 FPS). When coupled with downstream OCR engines, RT-DocLayout significantly improves full-document reconstruction quality, providing a scalable and practical foundation for real-world document intelligence systems.

</details>


### [258] [Faithful Grounded Visual Reasoning via Learned Proxy-Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23354)
*Tom Hodemon,Mohamed Chaouch,Aboubacar Tuo,Angelique Loesch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Composer，一种基于可学习代理令牌（proxy-tokens）的新型视觉定位机制的多模态大语言模型，通过离散符号指针显式索引图像潜空间，在保持答案精度的同时将视觉定位准确率提升9.0个百分点，解决了传统坐标定位方法中语义-空间鸿沟导致的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在VQA任务中表现出色，但其"黑箱"特性阻碍了在关键领域的部署。现有基于文本坐标的GVR方法缺乏与视觉特征的可学习语义连接，导致模型产生与图像证据不匹配的坐标幻觉，存在语义-空间鸿沟。

Method: 提出Composer模型，采用基于可学习代理令牌（proxy-tokens）的视觉定位机制。这些离散符号指针显式索引图像潜空间，将视觉区域作为可寻址、可语义操作的集合进行处理。同时构建了ComposerGCoT数据集用于全面评估推理一致性和定位准确度。

Result: Composer在最终答案准确率上与基于坐标的基线模型持平，同时视觉定位准确率提升9.0个百分点。离散代理令牌比传统文本坐标更有效地捕获空间语义。

Conclusion: 具有可学习语义连接的视觉定位机制是通往可信赖、可靠多模态大语言模型的有前景路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet their "black-box" nature hinders deployment in critical domains. Grounded Visual Reasoning (GVR) approaches attempt to improve interpretability by explicitly couple textual rationales with visual grounding information, which are typically textual coordinates. This mechanism lacks a learnable semantic link to the visual features, often resulting in a semantic-spatial gap where the model hallucinates coordinates that do not correspond to image evidences. In this work, we introduce Composer, a MLLM that leverages a novel visual grounding mechanism based on learned proxy-tokens to promote faithful interpretability. These discrete symbolic pointers explicitly index the image latent space, allowing the model to manipulate visual regions as addressable, semantically manipulable sets. To rigorously validate our novel grounding mechanism, we constructed ComposerGCoT, a dataset synthesized to enable holistic assessment of reasoning consistency and grounding accuracy. Experimental results indicate that Composer achieves performance parity with its coordinate-based counterpart in final answer accuracy, while improving visual grounding accuracy by +9.0 points. By demonstrating that discrete proxy-tokens capture spatial semantics more effectively than typical textual coordinates, we establish that visual grounding mechanisms with learnable semantic links represent a promising path toward trustworthy and reliable MLLMs.

</details>


### [259] [Changing Modalities: Adapting Remote Sensing Models to New Satellites and Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23356)
*Tim G. Zhou,Anthony Fuller,Geoff Pleiss,Evan Shelhamer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeluluNet架构，通过模态幻觉技术从单模态教师模型学习多模态表示，支持遥感模型在传感器变更时的三种场景：模态替换（迁移）、新增（超集）和减少（子集），无需重新标注数据即可适应输入模态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域机器学习模型通常基于固定模态集训练和部署，但新旧卫星传感器更替导致实际部署时面临模态替换、增加或减少的问题，传统做法需要大量重新标注和重新训练，成本高昂且不实用。

Method: 提出DeluluNet模块化架构，通过模态幻觉（modality hallucination）技术，利用单模态教师模型和未标注的多模态数据，端到端学习从现有模态预测缺失模态的表示，从而支持三种模态变化场景：模态迁移（替换）、模态增加（超集）和模态减少（子集）。

Result: DeluluNet能够在不重新标注和重新训练的情况下，持续对输入模态发生变化的样本进行预测，为动态变化的遥感应用场景提供了实用替代方案。

Conclusion: DeluluNet通过模块化设计和模态幻觉学习，有效解决了遥感模型部署中因传感器变更导致的模态不匹配问题，是一种无需重新标注数据即可适应模态变化的实用方案。

Abstract: Machine learning models for remote sensing are trained and deployed on a static set of modalities. However, as we equip newer satellites with novel sensors and retire old ones, practitioners may wish to deploy an existing model on a substitution, superset, or subset of modalities with minimal retraining given data availability or practical computational constraints. We study the setting of updating existing models to changing modalities and identify three main scenarios: Modality Transfer (substitution), Addition (superset), and Peeking (subset). We propose DeluluNet, an architecture with modular components for all three changing modality scenarios. DeluluNet is trained end-to-end, learning a multi-modal model from a unimodal teacher and unlabeled multimodal data via modality hallucination--predicting missing modality representations from those that are present. As a result, DeluluNet can keep predicting even when input modalities change, providing a practical alternative to re-labeling and re-training in a changing world.

</details>


### [260] [Polynomial Dice Loss for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23373)
*Hiroaki Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多项式Dice损失(Polynomial Dice Loss)，通过泰勒展开将Dice Loss表示为多项式形式，利用高阶分量调整来控制损失函数形状，在多个医学图像分割任务中取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割中存在数据不平衡和小病灶检测难题，现有Dice Loss虽能缓解这些问题但缺乏对损失函数形状的灵活控制能力，需要一种更通用的方法来调节高阶成分的贡献。

Method: 利用Dice Loss的几何特性，通过泰勒展开将其表示为多项式形式，通过调整高阶分量的贡献权重来控制损失函数形状，实现对不同分割难易程度的适应性。

Result: 在多个分割设置下，与基于传统Dice系数和Tversky系数的损失函数相比，多项式Dice Loss在性能上具有竞争力，且多项式形式提供了简单有效的损失形状控制方式。

Conclusion: 多项式扩展为Dice Loss提供了一种简单可控的改进方案，能有效应对医学图像分割中的数据不平衡和小病灶检测挑战，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a fundamental task for medical image processing and computer-assisted intervention, yet data imbalance and small lesion detection pose significant challenges. Dice Loss, which measures the overlap between predicted and ground truth regions, is widely used to mitigate these issues. To further emphasize its properties, we propose Polynomial Dice Loss, a polynomial extension of Dice Loss. Specifically, by leveraging the geometric characteristics of Dice Loss and formulating the loss function as a polynomial representation via Taylor expansion, we enable the adjustment of the contribution of higher-order components to the loss function. In our experiments, we evaluate the proposed method against loss functions derived from conventional Dice and Tversky coefficients. Experimental results and further analysis show that the polynomial formulation provides a simple way to control the loss shape and achieves competitive performance across multiple segmentation settings.

</details>


### [261] [Rethinking Object-Centric Representations for Video Dynamics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23436)
*Amaury Wei,Ismail Nejjar,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对无监督视频目标跟踪中外观与位姿纠缠导致的目标碎片化、身份交换等问题，提出STAITUS框架。该框架将每个slot显式解耦为外观和几何位姿，在外观空间施加时序对齐，并引入自适应门控机制动态调整slot数量。在合成及真实基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于slot的无监督视频目标跟踪方法强制对slot嵌入施加时序一致性，但由于外观与位姿信息纠缠，该一致性目标与目标运动及视角变化相冲突，导致slot倾向于锁定静态区域（如背景），而前景目标碎片化或频繁交换身份。

Method: 1) 将每个slot显式解耦为外观表示和几何位姿（位置/尺度）；2) 利用解耦后的表示，在帧内施加空间分离约束，仅在外观空间进行时序对齐；3) 引入自适应门控机制，动态调整活跃slot数量以匹配场景复杂度。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，STAITUS在分割质量和跟踪稳定性方面显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，尤其在运动、遮挡、目标进出场景等挑战下表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过外观与几何位姿的显式解耦以及自适应门控机制，有效解决了无监督视频目标跟踪中外观一致性约束与运动变化的冲突问题，提升了掩码锐度和身份持久性。

Abstract: Unsupervised video object tracking aims to decompose dynamic scenes into persistent, object-centric entities without manual annotations. Many recent approaches rely on slot-based representations, where a fixed set of latent variables ("slots") represent individual objects across frames. To preserve object identity, these models enforce temporal consistency on slot embeddings. However, when appearance and pose are entangled, this consistency objective conflicts with object motion and viewpoint changes. As a result, slots tend to lock onto static regions (e.g., background) to satisfy the consistency objective, while foreground objects become fragmented across multiple slots or frequently swap identities. To address these limitations, we propose STAITUS, a unified framework that explicitly disentangles each slot into appearance and geometric pose (position/scale). Leveraging this disentanglement, STAITUS enforces within-frame spatial separation and applies temporal alignment only in appearance space, yielding sharper masks and more persistent identities under motion, occlusion, and object entry/exit. Furthermore, to mitigate over-segmentation, we introduce an adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically adjusts the number of active slots to match scene complexity. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that STAITUS substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in segmentation quality and tracking stability.

</details>


### [262] [MeGAS: Thermomechanical Dynamic Gaussian Splatting for Thermophysical Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23455)
*Zesong Yang,Yuanhang Lei,Liyuan Cui,Yihang Chen,Jiaer Huang,Boming Zhao,Peter Yichen Chen,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeGAS将热力学相变动力学集成到3D高斯泼溅框架中，通过温度属性、热对流-扩散求解器和MPM动力学实现热物理现象的物理一致且视觉真实的合成，并提出了拓扑自适应高斯渲染策略以应对极端变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理集成神经渲染方法仅关注机械驱动动力学，忽略了温度这一基础但不可见的物理因素，无法处理熔化、凝固等热力学相变过程。

Method: 1) 提出热力学动态高斯泼溅表示，为3DGS增加温度属性；2) 结合热对流-扩散求解器与含相变MPM动力学；3) 提出拓扑自适应高斯渲染策略减少开裂和漂浮伪影。

Result: 实验表明MeGAS能产生物理一致的热力学行为，同时保持高保真度照片级真实渲染效果。

Conclusion: MeGAS将热力学相变融入3DGS框架，为构建物理集成世界模型迈进了重要一步。

Abstract: Recent advances integrate physically grounded Newtonian dynamics with neural rendering frameworks, narrowing the gap between photorealistic scene reconstruction and physics-based animation. However, existing approaches focus on mechanically driven dynamics while neglecting temperature, a fundamental yet invisible physical factor underlying phenomena such as melting, solidification, and other thermomechanical processes. In this paper, we propose MeGAS, a novel framework that incorporates thermomechanical phase-change dynamics into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Specifically, we propose a new thermomechanical dynamic Gaussian Splatting representation that augments 3DGS with temperature attributes and employs a heat advection-diffusion solver with MPM dynamics incorporating phase transitions, enabling physically plausible and visually realistic synthesis of thermophysical phenomena. Furthermore, a new topology-adaptive Gaussian rendering strategy is proposed to mitigate cracking and floaters under extreme deformation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MeGAS produces physically consistent thermomechanical behavior while maintaining high-fidelity photorealistic rendering, advancing toward physics-integrated world models.

</details>


### [263] [C^2GR: Coupled Comprehensive Generative Replay for a Continually Learnable Universal Segmentation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23473)
*Wei Li,Jingyang Zhang,Guoan Wang,Junzhi Ning,Yang Chen,Guang Yang,Lixu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医疗影像通用分割模型在任务增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，提出C²GR框架，通过贝叶斯联合扩散保持图像-掩码对应关系，利用关系感知统一提示同步机制协调生成器与分割器优化，在20个跨模态/目标的连续任务上仅比联合训练下降2.44%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用分割模型在跨模态、跨分割目标的连续任务增量学习中，由于任务间图像外观和分割目标同时发生偏移，仅用当前任务训练会导致对旧任务的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出C²GR框架，包含：(1) 贝叶斯联合扩散方法，将图像-掩码对应关系建模为条件分布并通过条件去噪优化；(2) 关系感知统一提示同步机制，通过共享任务关系感知提示同时调控生成器与分割器，实现两者优化同步。

Result: 在20个涵盖不同模态和分割目标的任务上，C²GR相比联合训练仅下降2.44%整体性能，有效缓解了并发偏移导致的遗忘问题。

Conclusion: C²GR通过生成式回放结合双模块同步优化，有效解决了医疗影像通用分割模型在任务增量学习中面临的外观与目标双重偏移下的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Universal segmentation models exhibit significant potential for diverse tasks involving different imaging modalities and segmentation objectives. Task-Incremental Learning provides a privacy-preserving approach to continually evolve a universal model on tasks from sequentially-arriving medical departments. However, training the model solely on the incoming task induces forgetting on past tasks, since consecutive tasks exhibit concurrent shifts in image appearance and segmentation objective. To address this problem, we propose a novel Coupled Comprehensive Generative Replay (C^2GR) framework that simultaneously synthesizes image-mask pairs of previous tasks to mitigate forgetting under concurrent appearance and objective shifts. This requires preserving image-mask correspondence for structure-realistic generation and bridging asynchronous optimization of the generator and segmentor for segmentation-oriented generation. Specifically, we propose a Bayesian Joint Diffusion (BJD) method that formulates the correspondence as conditional distributions optimized via conditional denoising. Furthermore, we develop a Relation-aware Unified Prompt Synchronization (RUPS) scheme to simultaneously modulate the generator and segmentor via a shared task-relation-aware prompt for synchronizing their optimization. Experiments on 20 tasks spanning diverse modalities and objectives demonstrate that C^2GR exhibits only a 2.44% drop in overall performance compared to joint training with all task data, effectively alleviating forgetting from the concurrent shifts. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/mar-cry/C2GR.

</details>


### [264] [From Reconstruction to Decision: A Post-Encoder Plug-in Adapter for Curvilinear Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23486)
*Qin Lei,Jiang Zhong,Xin Xiao,Yuming Yang,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PEPA，一种轻量级后编码器插件适配器，专门用于2D曲线状物体分割（如血管、裂纹），通过目标条件蛇形上采样（TCSU）和目标自适应可微分阈值化（TADT）解决高分辨率特征恢复中的重建瓶颈和二值化中的决策瓶颈，在仅增加约0.26M参数的情况下显著提升拓扑连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线状物体分割面临极端空间稀疏性和拓扑脆弱性，小局部错误会导致严重结构断裂。现代分割流水线依赖难以修改的基础编码器，其大幅下采样限制了细微结构的恢复。现有方法在编码器后阶段存在两个关键瓶颈：高分辨率特征恢复的重建瓶颈和二值化的决策瓶颈。

Method: PEPA包含两个核心模块：(1) 目标条件蛇形上采样（TCSU），通过目标条件的连续蛇形采样更好地恢复细长和弯曲结构；(2) 目标自适应可微分阈值化（TADT），预测目标特定阈值并优化软阈值代理，带有防止平凡偏置漂移的显式保护。该方法作为后编码器插件，可附加到基于提示的解码器和传统密集预测器上。

Result: 在五个医学和工业基准上，添加PEPA到冻结编码器基线带来一致改进，拓扑连通性（clDice）的提升通常超过区域重叠（IoU）的提升，表明结构连续性得到改善。仅需约0.26M额外参数。

Conclusion: PEPA提供了一种实用的后编码器增强方案，专门针对结构中心的分割任务，以极小参数代价显著提升曲线状物体的拓扑连通性。

Abstract: Curvilinear object segmentation, including vessels and cracks, is challenging due to extreme spatial sparsity and topological fragility, where small local errors can cause severe structural disconnections. Meanwhile, modern segmentation pipelines increasingly rely on strong but hard-to-modify foundation encoders whose heavy downsampling limits fine structural recovery. Motivated by this, we focus on the post-encoder stage and study two recurring and actionable failure modes: a reconstruction bottleneck in high-resolution feature restoration and a decision bottleneck in binarization. We present PEPA, a lightweight Post-Encoder Plug-in Adapter for 2D curvilinear segmentation pipelines with accessible decoder/head features and target, query, or class descriptors. PEPA couples (i) Target-Conditioned Snake Upsampling (TCSU), which uses target-conditioned continuous snake-like sampling to better recover thin and tortuous structures during upsampling, and (ii) Target-Adaptive Differentiable Thresholding (TADT), which predicts target-specific thresholds and optimizes a soft-threshold surrogate with explicit safeguards against trivial bias shifting. Under this post-encoder interface, PEPA can be attached to both prompt-based decoders and conventional dense predictors. Experiments on five medical and industrial benchmarks show that adding PEPA to frozen-encoder baselines yields consistent improvements, with gains in topological connectivity (clDice) typically exceeding those in region overlap (IoU), indicating improved structural continuity. With only $\sim$0.26M additional parameters, PEPA offers a practical post-encoder enhancement for structure-centric segmentation.

</details>


### [265] [Brain-Adapter: A Dual-Stream Vision-Language MIL Framework for Comprehensive 3D CT Diagnosis of Acute Intracranial Pathologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23494)
*Zhenyu Yi,Zhiyun Song,Yusong Sun,Zelin Liu,Manman Fei,Zhenhao Li,Jiaxuan Zhao,Xu Han,Lichi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain-Adapter，一种基于双流多实例学习的框架，利用预训练2D生物医学视觉语言模型和原始诊断报告，实现3D脑CT扫描的自动多标签分类，无需密集人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D脑CT扫描的自动诊断对重症监护至关重要，但现有方法严重依赖人工标注且传统模型语义理解有限。2D基础视觉语言模型虽有强大泛化能力，但如何将其表征能力有效迁移至3D体数据仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出Brain-Adapter双流多实例学习框架：(1) 文本条件注意力机制，以原始诊断句子为语义查询动态对齐视觉线索与疾病概念；(2) 并行视觉MIL流捕捉全局扫描特征，由LLM提取的结构化标签监督；(3) 一致性约束确保双流表征协同；(4) 推理时不确定性感知精化模块动态校准融合双流预测。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法显著优于现有最先进的3D模型和标准MIL方法。

Conclusion: Brain-Adapter消除了对密集标注的依赖，为3D急性颅内病理分析提供了高度可扩展且临床可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated diagnosis of 3D brain CT scans is essential for critical care, yet it remains challenging due to the heavy reliance on manual annotations and the limited semantic understanding of conventional models. While 2D foundation vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable generalization, effectively transferring their representational power to 3D volumes remains an open problem. In this paper, we propose Brain-Adapter, a novel dual-stream multiple instance learning (MIL) framework that leverages pre-trained 2D biomedical VLMs and raw diagnostic reports for robust scan-level multi-label classification. Specifically, we introduce a Text-Conditioned Attention (TCA) mechanism, utilizing raw diagnostic sentences as semantic queries to dynamically align visual cues with specific disease concepts. Concurrently, a parallel visual MIL stream captures global scan characteristics, supervised by structured labels extracted via a Large Language Model (LLM). To ensure representation coherence, a consistency constraint enforces synergy between the two streams. During inference, an Uncertainty-Aware Refinement (UAR) module dynamically calibrates and fuses these dual-stream predictions to resolve ambiguous cases. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D models and standard MIL approaches. By eliminating the reliance on dense annotations, Brain-Adapter provides a highly scalable and clinically viable solution for 3D acute intracranial pathology analysis.

</details>


### [266] [UniverSat: Resolution- and Modality-Agnostic Transformers for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23503)
*Yohann Perron,Guillaume Astruc,Nicolas Gonthier,Clement Mallet,Loic Landrieu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniverSat提出了一种通用补丁编码器（Universal Patch Encoder），能将来自不同空间、光谱、时间分辨率以及光学与非光学传感器的任意补丁映射到共享嵌入空间，实现单一模型在异质多模态地球观测数据上的自监督训练，在多个标准基准上取得优秀结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers (ViT) 在计算机视觉中占主导地位，但其依赖刚性补丁投影器，难以迁移到地球观测（EO）领域，因为EO的输入模态、尺度和分辨率变化广泛。现有方法需要针对不同传感器单独设计模型，缺乏统一的跨模态表示能力。

Method: 提出UniverSat，一种ViT风格骨干网络，核心是通用补丁编码器（Universal Patch Encoder），该编码器能将来自任意空间、光谱、时间分辨率的光学和非常规传感器补丁，通过共享权重映射到统一的嵌入空间中，从而支持在异质多模态语料库上通过自监督方式训练单一模型。

Result: 在GeoBench、PANGEABench和SpectralEarth等标准EO基准测试的分类和分割任务上取得了优异结果。

Conclusion: UniverSat通过通用补丁编码器实现了传感器无关的空间特征学习，证明了在异质多模态EO数据上训练单一自监督模型的可行性，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViT) dominate computer vision. However, their reliance on rigid patch projectors hinders transfer to Earth Observation (EO), where input modalities, scales, and resolutions vary widely. We introduce UniverSat, a ViT-style backbone built around a Universal Patch Encoder that maps patches from arbitrary spatial, spectral, and temporal resolutions, and from both optical and non-optical sensors, into a shared embedding space with a shared set of weights. This enables training a single model on heterogeneous multimodal corpora via self-supervision, yielding robust, sensor-agnostic spatial features. We validate this approach with strong results across classification and segmentation on standard EO benchmarks from GeoBench, PANGEABench, and SpectralEarth. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/gastruc/UniverSat.

</details>


### [267] [Arbor: Explicit Geometric Conditioning for Controllable 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23514)
*Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Andreas Engelhardt,Simon Donne,Hendrik P. A. Lensch,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Arbor 是一种可训练的附件模块，通过引入约束网格（包括存在区域、回避区域和接触区域）作为原生3D控制接口，在冻结的去噪器中学习路由附件，从而实现对文本条件潜在3D生成中空间意图的精确控制，无需专用损失函数即可提升约束服从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本和图像条件3D模型虽能生成令人信服的资产，但缺乏对物体应占据或回避空间的直接控制。提示词和图像视图难以承载这类空间约束，需要显式的控制接口。

Method: Arbor 将约束网格转换为token，并在冻结的扩散去噪器内部学习一个路由附件。每个潜在区域仅接收与其空间位置相关的约束部分。约束网格分为三类：船体区域（应存在几何）、回避区域（应保持空置）和接触区域（物体应接触）。

Result: 在自动和艺术家策划的控制基准测试中，Arbor 在船体、回避和接触约束下均提升了约束服从性，同时保持了物体质量和多样性。用户偏好研究与指标趋势一致。

Conclusion: Arbor 提供了一种有效的3D空间控制接口，即使在无专用服从损失的情况下也能显著提升约束遵守程度，且不牺牲生成质量与变化性。

Abstract: Text and image conditioned 3D models now generate convincing assets, but they still offer little direct control over the space an object should occupy or avoid. In authoring, this spatial intent is often known before generation starts. A chair should fit a seating envelope, a prop should leave clearance for motion, or a part should expose a contact surface. Prompts and image views are poor carriers for such constraints, requiring the need for an explicit control interface.
  We present Arbor, a trainable attachment for text conditioned latent 3D generation. Arbor introduces constraint meshes as a native 3D control interface. The interface uses hull regions where geometry should exist, avoidance regions that should remain empty, and touch regions the object should contact. Unlike completion or whole object scaffold control, these meshes are not target evidence. They are local typed requirements and can include regions where no surface should appear. Arbor keeps this signal as geometry by converting constraint meshes into tokens and learning a routed attachment inside a frozen denoiser. Each latent region can therefore receive the part of the constraint that matters for its spatial location.
  We evaluate Arbor on automatic and artist curated control benchmarks with hull, avoidance, and touch constraints, and compare the metric trends to a user preference study. Even without dedicated compliance losses, Arbor improves constraint obedience while preserving object quality and variation under fixed constraints.

</details>


### [268] [Scaling State-Space Models from Lines to Paragraphs: An Ablation of Mamba-based OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23524)
*Merveilles Agbeti-Messan,Pierrick Tranouez,Stéphane Nicolas,Clément Chatelain,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于Mamba的状态空间模型（SSM）在OCR识别中从行到段落文本的缩放行为，发现其在合成数据上速度快（1.4-4.5倍）且精度与Transformer相当，但在真实手写数据上精度明显落后（如IAM段落10.0% vs 3.5% CER），主要差距源于数据稀缺而非架构限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端OCR中Transformer自回归序列模型的二次复杂度限制了长段落文本的高效转录，而SSM（如Mamba）提供线性时间解码，但其在从短行到长段落、从印刷体到手写体的行为尚不明确。

Method: 系统探索Mamba OCR识别器的四个核心超参数（解码器深度、状态维度、扩展因子、连接器深度）在100-1000字符合成段落上的表现，并与相同训练协议的Transformer基线进行全面对比。

Result: 合成数据上两者均保持低于1% CER，SSM速度快1.4-4.5倍且优势随序列长度增加；真实手写数据上SSM明显落后（IAM行8.2%、段落10.0% CER vs Transformer的4.2%、3.5%），差距主要源于数据饥饿问题。

Conclusion: SSM在大规模文档转录中的适用性取决于数据条件：数据充足时线性解码速度优势明显，但数据稀缺时精度远不如Transformer。

Abstract: End-to-end OCR increasingly relies on autoregressive sequence models, where the quadratic cost of Transformer attention limits efficient transcription of long, paragraph-level text. State-Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba offer linear-time decoding and have recently been shown to match Transformer accuracy on printed historical lines, but their behavior as sequences grow from short lines to full paragraphs, and their generalization to handwriting, remain poorly understood. We study how a Mamba-based OCR recognizer scales from lines to paragraphs. We first conduct a systematic exploration of its four core hyperparameters (decoder depth, state dimension, expansion factor, and connector depth) on synthetic paragraphs from 100 to 1,000 characters, identifying the recurrent state dimension and the expansion factor as the dominant levers for long-sequence accuracy. We then compare the recognizer against a Transformer baseline trained under an identical protocol. On clean synthetic paragraphs, both models stay below 1% CER at every length while the SSM runs 1.4 to 4.5 times faster, the speedup growing with sequence length. On real handwriting, however, the SSM lags clearly behind: it reaches 8.2% CER on IAM lines and 10.0% on IAM paragraphs, against 4.2% and 3.5% for the Transformer baseline. Through controlled experiments we show that a substantial part of this gap stems from data scarcity rather than from an intrinsic architectural limit: the autoregressive SSM decoder is markedly data-hungry on long sequences. Our study clarifies when SSMs are a practical choice for large-scale document transcription and when they are not.

</details>


### [269] [LightSTAR: Efficient Visual Document Retrieval via Lightweight Selection with Vision-Adaptive Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23539)
*Tongkun Guan,Haocheng Wang,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightSTAR提出了一种高效的视觉文档检索框架，将过程分解为LLM-free视觉选择（基于内容查询编码和视觉嵌入快速筛选候选集）和视觉自适应语义精炼（对候选集进行细粒度语义匹配），在保持SOTA准确率的同时将端到端延迟降低数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视觉文档检索方法虽然准确率高，但对每一页都进行密集的MLLM编码，计算成本过高。用户查询通常包含关键词锚定，这些语义丰富的词应直接出现在相关页面的可见文本中，可作为高效筛选候选页面的线索。

Method: LightSTAR将视觉文档检索分解为两个阶段：1) LLM-free Visual Selection，利用基于内容的查询编码聚焦信息性词汇，通过无LLM的视觉嵌入生成高召回率候选集；2) Vision-adaptive Semantic Refinement，仅对候选集进行细粒度语义匹配，通过自适应区域特征融合结合文本与布局线索，并采用难度感知对比目标优化。

Result: 实验结果表明LightSTAR取得了最先进的检索准确率，同时将端到端延迟降低数倍，有效平衡了视觉文档检索中的准确率与效率。

Conclusion: LightSTAR通过两阶段分解策略为视觉文档检索提供了一种高实用性的解决方案，在准确性与效率之间取得了优良的平衡。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval requires rapidly locating relevant pages from large multi-modal corpora in response to user queries. While recent methods powered by Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show competitive accuracy, they suffer from prohibitive computational costs by applying intensive MLLM encoding to every single page. Meanwhile, we observe that user queries are typically keyword-anchored, containing semantically rich words that are expected to appear directly in the visible text of relevant pages, offering an efficient cue for quickly narrowing down candidate pages. Building on this insight, we propose LightSTAR, an efficient framework that decomposes visual document retrieval into: 1) LLM-free Visual Selection, which utilizes content-grounded query encoding to focus on informative words and employs LLM-free visual embeddings to produce a high-recall candidate set; and 2) Vision-adaptive Semantic Refinement, which further performs fine-grained semantic matching exclusively on these top candidates via adaptive region-wise feature fusion to effectively combine textual and layout cues, optimized through a hardness-aware contrastive objective. Experimental results demonstrate that LightSTAR achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy while reducing end-to-end latency by several-fold, offering a highly practical solution to the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in visual document retrieval. Code is available at https://github.com/bokufa/LightSTAR.

</details>


### [270] [AwakeForest: An Interactive Geospatial Platform for Large-Scale Forest Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23542)
*Suraj Prasai,Kangning Cui,Rongkun Zhu,Sarra Alqahtani,Ying Zhang,Victor Paul Pauca,Miles R. Silman,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AwakeForest是一个面向大规模森林影像的交互式端到端平台，集成模型辅助推理、自动标注和人在回路精炼，支持云端优化的地理空间数据分析和即插即用预训练模型，可处理从标准航片到数百GB正射影像的数据，并生成可直接用于下游分析的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林影像分析涉及多个紧密耦合的视觉任务，面临地理区域、传感器和采集条件的巨大变化，而现有工具缺乏统一的地理空间原生、云端优化和ML集成的端到端工作流支持。

Method: 提出AwakeForest平台，采用交互式端到端设计，集成模型辅助推理、自动标注和人在回路精炼，支持预训练模型的即插即用，可扩展处理从标准航片到数百GB正射影像的数据。

Result: 在PALMS数据集上展示了系统支持端到端工作流的能力，可用于实际的森林管理和分析任务。

Conclusion: AwakeForest为森林影像分析提供了一个统一、可扩展且实用的端到端平台，有效解决了地理空间数据处理中的规模化挑战。

Abstract: Forest imagery analysis often involves multiple tightly coupled vision tasks, which must be performed under substantial variation in geographic regions, sensors, and acquisition conditions. However, practitioners often lack a unified tool that is geospatial-native, cloud-optimized, and ML-integrated for end-to-end workflows spanning annotation, prediction, visualization, and downstream analysis at scale. We present AwakeForest, an interactive end-to-end platform designed for large-scale forest imagery that integrates model-assisted inference, automatic annotation, and human-in-the-loop refinement within a single workflow. Our platform supports plug-and-play integration of pretrained models and enables scalable interaction with forest imagery ranging from standard aerial scenes to large orthomosaics that can span several gigabytes to hundreds of gigabytes. AwakeForest produces analysis-ready outputs that can be directly used for downstream analysis and to support iterative model and annotation updates on new scenes. We demonstrate the system on the PALMS dataset and illustrate how AwakeForest supports an end-to-end workflow for practical forest management and analysis.

</details>


### [271] [Dense Reward for Multi-View 3D Reasoning with Global Maps and Local Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23557)
*Jiho Choi,Seonho Lee,Seojeong Park,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DR-MV3D 提出了一种基于地图的密集奖励学习框架，通过全局一致性奖励和局部轨迹奖励来监督多视图3D VQA的推理过程，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在多视图3D VQA中仅使用稀疏的答案级监督，导致跨视图推理不一致和视图选择脆弱，缺乏对中间推理过程的有效监督。

Method: 将MV3D-VQA分解为三个可学习的中间步骤：异中心全局地图构建、问题条件化的视角轨迹规划、以及自我中心接地用于答案预测。引入两种密集奖励：全局一致性奖励（通过冻结的3D视觉基础模型对齐预测地图与几何一致伪目标）和局部轨迹奖励（监督有序视角选择）。使用轨迹级策略优化（GRPO）优化整个流程。

Result: 在MindCube、VSI-Bench和BLINK (MV)三个基准上，DR-MV3D持续优于强多图像基线方法，证明了过程级密集监督对多视图3D推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 过程级密集奖励监督能够有效提升多视图3D视觉问答的推理一致性，为多步空间推理提供了可行的学习范式。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D Visual Question Answering (MV3D-VQA) requires integrating partial observations into a coherent 3D scene representation and selecting informative viewpoints for multi-step spatial reasoning. However, current multimodal LLMs are typically trained with sparse, answer-level supervision, which often yields inconsistent cross-view reasoning and brittle view selection. We present DR-MV3D (Dense Reward for MV3D-VQA), a map-grounded learning framework that provides dense, verifiable rewards to supervise the reasoning process. Our approach decomposes MV3D-VQA into (i) allocentric global map construction, (ii) question-conditioned view-trajectory planning, and (iii) egocentric grounding for answer prediction. To make intermediate steps learnable without manual annotations, we introduce two rewards: a global consistency reward that aligns the predicted map with geometry-consistent pseudo targets from frozen 3D vision foundation models (e.g., VGGT + SAM3), and a local trajectory reward that supervises ordered viewpoint selection. We optimize the full pipeline with trajectory-level policy optimization (GRPO). Experiments on MindCube, VSI-Bench, and BLINK (MV) show that DR-MV3D consistently improves over strong multi-image baselines, supporting the effectiveness of process-level dense supervision for multi-view 3D reasoning.

</details>


### [272] [Polycepta: Object-Centric Appearance Estimation for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23604)
*Mohamed Nagy,Naoufel Werghi,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Polycepta提出了一种以对象为中心的外观状态估计框架，将外观建模重构为递归估计问题，通过持续更新每个跟踪对象的独立外观状态来提升多目标跟踪性能，在KITTI、Waymo Open Dataset和MOT17上一致减少了身份切换，在KITTI基准上达到92.27% MOTA，运行速度90.57 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的tracking-by-detection范式中，外观描述子是帧独立的静态表示，缺乏鲁棒性；而实时MOT系统为了速度常完全抛弃外观线索，仅依赖运动预测和几何关联。作者希望将外观建模从帧级匹配任务重构为递归估计问题，以提升外观表示的鲁棒性和时效性。

Method: Polycepta为每个跟踪目标构建并持续更新独立的外观状态，将未来外观表示视为从累积观测中估计的结果；通过提出的学习策略鼓励模型学习外观状态的构造方式而非记忆具体表示，从而支持对未见类别的外观估计；外观估计质量随推理过程中目标状态的演化而逐步改善。

Result: 在KITTI、Waymo Open Dataset和MOT17上，集成Polycepta后一致减少了身份切换并提升了跟踪性能；集成到RobMOT框架后在KITTI基准上达到92.27% MOTA，运行速度90.57 Hz，达到当时最优水平。

Conclusion: Polycepta证明了将外观建模视为递归估计问题比传统静态描述子更具优势，外观估计质量可随观测累积而渐进式提升，且能泛化到未见类别，为实时MOT系统提供了高效且鲁棒的外观建模新范式。

Abstract: The tracking-by-detection paradigm in multi-object tracking (MOT) typically relies on static appearance descriptors to complement motion estimation. However, these descriptors are frame-independent, limiting their robustness as visual cues. Since such descriptors are often obtained from computationally intensive pretrained backbones, real-time MOT systems frequently abandon appearance cues altogether and rely solely on motion prediction and geometric association. In this work, we introduce Polycepta, an object-centric appearance state estimation framework that reformulates appearance modeling as a recursive estimation problem rather than a frame-wise matching task. Polycepta constructs and continuously updates an independent appearance state for each tracked object, enabling future appearance representations to be estimated from accumulated observations. Polycepta is encouraged to learn the appearance-state construction of object-specific representations rather than memorize them through a proposed learning strategy, enabling appearance estimation for unseen classes. A key property of Polycepta is that the quality of appearance estimation improves as object states evolve during inference. While conventional appearance descriptors remain static or degrade over time, Polycepta progressively refines appearance estimates as additional observations are accumulated. Extensive experiments on KITTI, the Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 demonstrate consistent reductions in identity switches and improvements in tracking performance when integrated into the tracking-by-detection pipelines. Polycepta operates at 90.57 Hz and delivers state-of-the-art performance on the KITTI benchmark when integrated into the RobMOT framework, achieving a MOTA of 92.27\%.

</details>


### [273] [Vera: A Layered Diffusion Model for Content-Preserving Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23610)
*Hongkai Zheng,Ta-Ying Cheng,Benjamin Klein,Yisong Yue,Zhuoning Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vera是一种分层扩散框架，通过生成编辑层和alpha遮罩与源视频合成，利用MoT架构和高质量分层数据集，在不改变源视频内容的前提下实现视频编辑，在内容保留方面优于现有开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在编辑时会重新生成每一帧像素，经常改变本应保留的内容（如角色或背景场景），内容保留是视频编辑的核心挑战。

Method: 提出Vera分层扩散框架：(1) 生成编辑层和alpha遮罩与源视频合成，分离创意编辑和内容保留；(2) 将文本到视频DiT扩展为Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT)架构，各层DiT通过联合自注意力交互；(3) 构建高质量分层数据集，包含精确alpha遮罩、多样场景和视觉特效。

Result: 在定量基准和人类偏好研究中，Vera仅使用486K帧分层训练数据，在内容保留方面优于领先的开源视频编辑模型，同时在编辑质量上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过分层生成和合成机制，Vera有效解决了视频编辑中的内容保留问题，为内容保留型视频编辑提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Video diffusion models have enabled remarkable progress in video generation and editing. However, content preservation remains a core challenge: existing methods regenerate every pixel and often alter elements that should remain unchanged, such as characters or background scenes. We introduce Vera, a layered diffusion framework for content-preserving video editing. Instead of regenerating the entire video, Vera generates an edit layer along with an alpha matte for compositing with the source video, separating creative editing from content preservation by design. To encourage coherent composition with the source video, we extend the text-to-video DiT into a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, with separate DiTs for each layer that interact through joint self-attention. To support the training of Vera, we further construct a high-quality layered dataset with accurate alpha mattes, diverse scenes and dynamics, and visual effects. Across our quantitative benchmark and human preference study, Vera outperforms leading open-source video editing models in content preservation while remaining competitive in edit quality, using 486K frames of layered training data.

</details>


### [274] [Data Selection Through Iterative Self-Filtering for Vision-Language Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23611)
*Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu,Sarvjeet Singh Ghotra,Morgane M. Moss,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Filtering自举方法，通过CLIP模型在自选的动态数据集上迭代训练，平衡高质量干净样本与多样本，无需额外数据或预训练模型即可提升视觉-语言模型的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言数据集中噪声严重，人工审核不可行，现有去噪方法依赖启发式规则、人工标注参考数据集或预训练模型，缺乏自动化且高效的过滤机制。

Method: 提出Self-Filtering自举方法：在CLIP模型训练过程中迭代进行数据选择，构建动态演化的数据集，平衡过滤后的高置信度干净样本与来自全分布中的多样样本。

Result: 在视觉-语言数据集上应用该方法过滤后训练，无需额外数据或预训练模型，即提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: Self-Filtering方法能有效自动过滤噪声数据，是一种无需外部资源的数据净化方案，可提升视觉-语言模型性能。

Abstract: The availability of large amounts of clean data is paramount to training neural networks. However, at large scales, manual oversight is impractical, resulting in sizeable datasets that can be very noisy. Attempts to mitigate this obstacle to producing performant vision-language models have so far involved heuristics, curated reference datasets, and using pre-trained models. Here we propose a novel, bootstrapped method in which a CLIP model is trained on an evolving, self-selected dataset. This evolving dataset constitutes a balance of filtered, highly probable clean samples as well as diverse samples from the entire distribution. Our proposed Self-Filtering method iterates between training the model and selecting a subsequently improved data mixture. Training on vision-language datasets filtered by the proposed approach improves downstream performance without the need for additional data or pre-trained models.

</details>


### [275] [Hedgementation = Hedgerow Segmentation: A Remote Sensing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23615)
*Nathan Senyard,Salem Hamdani,Astrid Zhang,Derek Wang,Evan Shelhamer,Mathias Lécuyer,Joséphine Gantois*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Hedgementation基准，用于评估机器学习模型在全法国尺度下以10m²空间分辨率进行树篱测绘的能力，融合多遥感数据与地面标签，测试了三种基线模型在空间距离和气候区上的泛化能力，涵盖监督与自监督学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感树篱测绘缺乏统一的大规模评估基准，特别是缺乏对跨空间距离和跨气候区泛化能力的系统评测。

Method: 整合并协调多种遥感数据产品与法国树篱清单的地面真值标签，构建基准数据集；采用三种基线模型，测试其在空间距离和气候区上的泛化能力；同时评估监督学习和自监督学习两种范式。

Result: 提出了首个面向国家尺度、10m²分辨率的树篱测绘基准，提供了基线模型的泛化性能评估结果。

Conclusion: Hedgementation基准为高农业重要性的细粒度特征跟踪提供了标准化评测平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose Hedgementation: a new benchmark to evaluate machine learning models for hedgerow mapping from remote sensing data at country scale and 10m$^2$ spatial resolution. We combine and harmonize multiple remote sensing data products and ground truth labels sourced from a hedgerow inventory in France. We measure the ability of three baseline models to generalize across spatial distance, and across climatic zones, a more explicitly challenging task. Our benchmark tests both supervised and self-supervised learning approaches for remote sensing, applied to tracking fine-scale features of high agricultural importance. The code to reproduce the benchmark and baselines results is available at https://github.com/hedgementation/hedgementation.

</details>


### [276] [Pose Anything Anywhere:Model-free Object Poses from Arbitrary References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23634)
*Hongli Xu,Jiaqi Hu,Junwen Huang,Boyang Zhong,Peter KT Yu,Nassir Navab,Benjamin Busam,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PANY是一个统一的无模型6D位姿估计框架，支持RGB和RGB-D输入，可在单张或稀疏无位姿参考视图上运行，通过对新物体泛化实现跨视角几何一致性学习，超越传统成对匹配方法，在YCB-V和LM-O基准上分别提升12%和20%以上的位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型方法受限于成对单锚点匹配，在遮挡、大视角变化和低查询-参考重叠情况下表现不佳；而基于模型的方法依赖CAD资产或繁重的预注册流程，难以适用于开放世界场景。

Method: 基于多视图Transformer几何骨干网络，学习视角一致的几何特征和跨视角对齐线索；当辅助视图可用时，通过位姿图规范注册聚合多视图信息以增强几何覆盖，最终直接回归6D位姿。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA，相比现有无模型方法在YCB-V上位姿精度提升+12%，在LM-O上提升超过+20%，且在单参考和稀疏参考设置下均表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: PANY证明了通过多视图几何一致性学习和跨视图线索聚合，无模型方法可以在无需CAD模型的前提下，实现高精度、强鲁棒的6D位姿估计，为开放世界机器人感知提供了有效范本。

Abstract: Estimating the 6D pose of unseen objects is a fundamental yet challenging problem for open-world robotics and embodied perception. Model-based methods are accurate but depend on CAD assets or heavy onboarding, while most model-free approaches are still limited to pairwise single-anchor matching and thus fail under occlusion and large viewpoint changes with low query-reference overlap. Therefore, we present PANY, a unified model-free framework that seamlessly supports both RGB and RGB-D inputs, operates on one or sparse pose-free reference views, and generalizes effectively to novel objects. Built on a multi-view transformer geometry backbone, PANY moves beyond pairwise matching by learning view-consistent geometry and cross-view alignment cues that remain stable under wide baselines and limited overlap. When additional unposed assist views are available, PANY aggregates them via pose-graph canonical registration to increase geometric coverage and reinforce the final pose. Extensive experiments show that PANY achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, substantially outperforming existing model-free methods, improving pose accuracy by +12% on YCB-V and over +20% on LM-O. Furthermore, PANY consistently performs well under both single-reference and sparse-reference settings, demonstrating strong robustness in real-world environments.

</details>


### [277] [Lightweight Neural Framework for Robust 3D Volume and Surface Estimation from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23653)
*Diego E. Farchione,Ramzi Idoughi,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种全前馈框架，直接从多视图图像回归尺度归一化的体积和表面积及其不确定性，通过融合3D点云和2D特征绕过迭代优化，在低视角和稀疏数据场景下 outperforms 现有方法，并验证于珊瑚监测、饮食分析和人体测量学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体积和表面积估计方法计算成本高，且在稀疏噪声数据下表现不佳，亟需一种高效、鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 采用全前馈架构，通过图解码器融合3D点云重建与视图对齐的2D特征，直接回归尺度归一化的体积、表面积及其不确定性，无需迭代优化。

Result: 该方法在低输入图像数量下显著优于现有最先进方法，并在珊瑚监测、饮食分析和人体测量学三个应用中验证了其鲁棒性和适应性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种高速、可扩展的几何估计替代方案，在资源受限或稀疏视角场景下仍能保持高性能，适用于广泛的定量形状分析任务。

Abstract: Accurate volume and surface area estimation is critical for diverse applications, from marine ecology to medical diagnostics. However, existing methods often suffer from high computational costs and poor performance with sparse and noisy data. We propose a fully feed-forward framework that regresses scale-normalized volume and surface area and their associated uncertainties directly from multi-view images. By fusing 3D point cloud reconstructions with view-aligned 2D features through a graph-based decoder, our model bypasses iterative optimization, ensuring exceptional scalability and rapid inference. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly when operating with a low number of input images. Validated across coral monitoring, dietary analysis, and anthropometry, our proposed framework provides a robust, adaptable solution for quantitative shape analysis. This architecture provides a high-speed, scalable alternative for precise geometric estimation from visual data, maintaining high performance even in resource-constrained or sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [278] [GeoFidelity-Bench: Evaluating Segment-Level Geographic Fidelity in Text-to-Image Street-View Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23669)
*Kaizhen Tan,Hanzhe Hong,Siru Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFidelity-Bench是一个评估街景生成模型地理忠实度的基准，包含7,117张来自109个道路段的图像。研究发现，添加街道和社区名称能提升检索准确率5.5个百分点，但模型仍难以精确生成特定道路段，存在"城市/社区级合理"与"路段级忠实"之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型能生成视觉合理的城市街景，但无法确定其输出是否对应特定道路段而非通用城市先验。作者希望量化这种地理忠实度的差距。

Method: 构建GeoFidelity-Bench基准，包含109个命名道路段的7,117张Mapillary图像。采用参考面板排序方法，将目标段与同城最近段、同城其他段、其他城市段进行区分。评估6个开源文生图模型，使用城市级、街道+社区级、GPS增强三种提示策略。

Result: 添加街道和社区名称使top-1检索准确率提升5.5个百分点（95% CI: 3.4-7.7）。但目标段与同城最近段的相似度边际接近零。错误街道名仍能获得部分增益。GPS坐标作为文本无统计显著收益。真实图像查询能成功恢复路段身份。

Conclusion: 当前模型在生成城市或社区级合理的街景方面有进步，但在精确生成特定道路段方面仍存在显著差距，GeoFidelity-Bench揭示了这一忠实度鸿沟。

Abstract: Text-to-image models can generate visually plausible city streets, but whether their outputs correspond to a requested road segment rather than a generic city prior remains unclear. We introduce GeoFidelity-Bench, a reference-panel benchmark for segment-conditioned geographic fidelity in street-view generation. It contains 7,117 curated Mapillary images covering 109 named OpenStreetMap road segments in 25 cities across six continents. For each generated panel, the benchmark ranks the target reference panel against panels from the nearest segment in the same city, other segments in the same city, and segments from other cities, making local discrimination rather than absolute target similarity the primary test. We evaluate six open-weight text-to-image generators under city-only, street-and-neighborhood, and GPS-augmented prompts. Adding street and neighborhood names is associated with an increase of 5.5 percentage points in top-1 retrieval accuracy over city-only prompts, with a 95% confidence interval from 3.4 to 7.7 percentage points. However, the similarity margin between the target and the nearest segment in the same city remains near zero, indicating that local names improve broad local plausibility more than exact segment identity. Prompts that keep the city fixed but use incorrect street or neighborhood names further show that only part of the gain depends on the correct local names, while appending raw GPS coordinates as ordinary text yields no statistically clear additional benefit. Held-out real-image queries successfully recover segment identity, showing that the curated references contain usable segment-level signal. GeoFidelity-Bench thus reveals a persistent gap between city- or neighborhood-plausible street-view generation and faithful generation for a specific road segment.

</details>


### [279] [IMAGIN-4D: Image-Guided Controllable Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23675)
*Sai Kumar Dwivedi,Federica Bogo,Buğra Tekin,Chenhongyi Yang,Nadine Bertsch,Tomas Hodan,Michael J. Black,Dimitrios Tzionas,Shreyas Hampali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMAGIN-4D提出了一种基于扩散模型的HOI生成方法，通过时空解耦的图像条件化（空间交互状态token+时间帧感知token）以及角色感知的条件融合机制，利用参考图像解决交互歧义性，在FBM和BEHAVE数据集上实现了更精细的交互控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI生成方法依赖文本、物体几何和稀疏路点作为控制信号，但这些信号对交互的描述不够充分，同一提示和轨迹可能产生多种不同的抓取方式、接近方向、身体/物体姿态、接触点和布局。这种歧义性需要通过参考图像来提供更明确的交互视觉规范。

Method: 1) 空间条件：提取监督式交互状态token（身体姿态、物体姿态、身体-物体接触、空间关系）；2) 时间条件：计算帧感知token，每帧查询图像不同区域；3) 角色感知条件融合：文本/路点/交互状态token使用独立AdaLN流，帧感知token与运动token交叉注意力；4) 合成运动到图像的渲染管线以构建配对数据。

Result: 在FBM和BEHAVE数据集上的实验表明，IMAGIN-4D在细粒度交互控制上优于单token和均匀图像条件化的基线方法，同时保持路点跟随能力和运动质量。

Conclusion: 时空解耦的图像条件化策略能够有效解决参考图像中多种交互线索的混叠问题，为HOI生成提供了更精细的控制能力。

Abstract: Generating human-object interactions (HOI) is central to character animation, robotics, AR/VR, and embodied AI. Recent HOI generation methods synthesize motion from text, object geometry, and sparse waypoints, controlling action semantics and object trajectories. However, these signals underspecify interaction: the same prompt and trajectory can produce different grasps, approach directions, body poses, object poses, contacts, and body-object layouts. We address this ambiguity with a reference image as a visual specification of the desired interaction snapshot. However, a single global image representation conflates distinct cues and conditions all frames on identical visual evidence. We therefore introduce IMAGIN-4D, a diffusion-based HOI generator that decomposes image conditioning spatio-temporally. For spatial conditioning, IMAGIN-4D extracts supervised interaction-state tokens for body pose, object pose, body-object contact, and spatial relationships at the depicted frame. For temporal conditioning, it computes frame-aware tokens by querying image patches per generated frame, allowing sequence segments to attend to different visual cues from the same image. To balance image, text, and waypoint cues, IMAGIN-4D uses role-aware conditioning: text, waypoints, and interaction-state tokens use separate AdaLN streams, while frame-aware visual tokens cross-attend with motion tokens. Since HOI motion datasets lack paired images, we build a synthetic motion-to-image rendering pipeline from FullBodyManipulation (FBM) and introduce an image-adherence metric to evaluate whether generated motions match the reference snapshot. Experiments on FBM and BEHAVE show that IMAGIN-4D improves fine-grained interaction control over single-token and uniformly image-conditioned baselines while preserving waypoint-following and motion quality. Code and models will be released at https://imagin4d.github.io.

</details>


### [280] [AIR: Adaptive Interleaved Reasoning with Code in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23678)
*Cong Han,Xiaohan Lan,Haibo Qiu,Yujie Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展强化学习训练，赋予多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在代码增强的复杂数值计算任务上的自适应交织推理能力，提出包含两阶段冷启动数据构建、数据过滤策略和基于组约束奖励函数的自适应工具调用策略的三组件解决方案，在评估基准上平均提升6.1个百分点，交织推理样本准确率提升9.9个百分点，工具使用成功率超过95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注视觉感知任务中的工具使用，但这类方法依赖预定义的视觉操作启发式规则，无法处理数值计算问题，因为它们只专注于视觉操作。因此，需要赋予MLLMs在复杂数值计算任务上的自适应交织推理能力。

Method: 提出三组件解决方案：(1) 两阶段冷启动数据构建流水线；(2) 用于强化学习数据集筛选的数据过滤策略；(3) 利用组约束奖励函数的自适应工具调用策略，用于交织推理轨迹的生成。通过扩展强化学习训练在代码增强的复杂数值计算任务上进行训练。

Result: 经过基于组约束奖励函数的强化学习训练后，评估基准上性能平均提升6.1个百分点，交织推理样本准确率提升9.9个百分点，工具使用整体成功率超过95%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效提升了MLLMs在数值计算任务上的交织推理能力，验证了强化学习结合组约束奖励函数在代码增强推理中的有效性，为多模态大模型在复杂计算任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Following the paradigm shift initiated by OpenAI o3, interleaved reasoning with code to enhance multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has become a pivotal research frontier. The existing literature focuses primarily on tool-use within vision-perception tasks. However, such approaches typically rely on predefined heuristics for visual manipulation and are inherently incapable of addressing numerical computation problems due to their exclusive focus on visual operations. This paper empowers MLLMs with adaptive interleaved reasoning capabilities through extended reinforcement learning training on code-augmented complex numerical computation tasks. To this end, we propose a comprehensive three-component solution consisting of: a two-stage cold-start data construction pipeline, data filtering strategies for RL dataset curation, and an adaptive tool-invocation strategy leveraging a group-constrained reward function for interleaved reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that after Reinforcement Learning training with the group-constrained reward function, performance improves by an average of 6.1 percentage points (pp) on evaluation benchmarks. Specifically, the accuracy for interleaved reasoning samples increases by 9.9 pp, and the overall success rate of tool-use exceeds 95%. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/CongHan0808/AIR.git.

</details>


### [281] [Semantic Browsing: Controllable Diversity for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23679)
*Sara Dorfman,Maya Vishnevsky,Omer Dahary,Or Patashnik,Daniel Cohen-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控多样性的文本到图像生成方法，通过在大语言模型层面进行语义决策而非依赖随机噪声，实现了用户可导航的结构化图像画廊，使每个变体对应可理解的语义选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型虽然视觉保真度高，但生成样本多样性差，倾向于坍缩为单一视觉解释。现有多样性方法产生的输出由偶然变化驱动，而非有意义的设计选择，因此需要一种在生成样本上施加结构约束的多样性任务。

Method: 利用现代文本到图像模型训练于详细描述这一事实，将语义决策与像素生成解耦。直接在文本层面引入多样性：使用Vision Language Model (VLM) 在完整场景上下文中操作，并通过智能体工作流显式强制执行与原始提示相适应的结构化变化。

Result: 该方法能够生成多样且可导航的设计空间，其中每个变化都对应一个特定的、用户可理解的语义决策。

Conclusion: 通过将多样性控制从像素层面转移到文本语义层面，结合VLM和智能体工作流，可以实现有意义的、结构化的多样性生成，支持语义浏览这一新的创意探索范式。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models excel in visual fidelity and prompt adherence. However, this strict adherence comes at the cost of diversity: generated samples tend to collapse into a single visual interpretation. Existing methods to improve diversity produce outputs driven by incidental variations rather than meaningful design choices. This motivates a new variant of the diversity task where structure is enforced on the generated samples. We introduce a method for controlled diversity that enables Semantic Browsing, where users can navigate structured image galleries and experience creative exploration through a systematic traversal of meaningful, interpretable axes of variation. Achieving this level of semantic control requires a deep understanding of the scene. We exploit the fact that recent text-to-image models are trained on elaborated captions, effectively decoupling semantic decision-making from pixel generation. This enables a paradigm shift: instead of relying on stochastic variation within the text-to-image model, we induce diversity directly at the text level. By leveraging rich textual representations, we allow a Vision Language Model (VLM) to operate on the full scene context. To overcome the generic outputs typical of standard VLMs, we employ an agentic workflow that explicitly enforces structured variation attuned to the original prompt. We demonstrate that our method produces diverse and navigable design spaces where every variation corresponds to a specific, user-understandable semantic decision.

</details>


### [282] [Keep The Essentials: Efficient Reference Conditioned Generation via Token Dropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23682)
*Rishubh Parihar,Ayush Raina,R. Venkatesh Babu,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse Context方法，通过稀疏化参考token表示（保留约1/4 tokens）并采用随机token丢弃训练策略，在不损失视觉质量的情况下，实现多参考图像生成4倍加速、单参考2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考图像扩散模型推理计算成本高，且随参考图像数量严重扩展；现有高效扩散模型研究主要集中于纯文本条件生成，未解决参考模型特有的参考token冗余问题（密集token网格表示效率低）。

Method: 1) 推理时随机丢弃大量参考token仍保持生成能力；2) 采用随机token丢弃（不同比例）微调模型，使其鲁棒于部分参考表示；3) 推理时采用任务感知的token选择策略（非随机丢弃），优先保留参考图像中最具信息量的区域。

Result: 多参考图像生成速度提升4倍，单参考生成速度提升2倍；在空间对齐编辑和主体驱动生成任务中均不牺牲视觉质量。

Conclusion: 稀疏化参考token表示是提升参考图像扩散模型效率的有效途径，通过随机丢弃训练解耦特定选择规则，结合任务感知推理策略可在保证质量的同时实现显著加速。

Abstract: Reference-based diffusion models enable highly controllable image generation by leveraging elements from input images to guide prompt-driven synthesis. However, these models are computationally expensive in runtime, and their cost scales severely with the number of input references. While the efficiency of diffusion models has been extensively studied in the context of prompt-driven generation, it remains largely under-explored in the realm of reference-based models. This setting presents unique challenges not addressed by methods focusing solely on generation. In particular, the wasteful representation of references as dense token grids offers significant opportunities for improvement. In this work, we present Sparse Context, a method for constructing sparse reference representations by retaining only a reduced subset of reference tokens. We observe that even without modifying the model, dropping a significant portion of reference tokens at inference time largely preserves its generation capabilities. To fully realize this potential, we fine-tune the model with random token dropping at varying ratios, encouraging robustness to partial reference representations. Crucially, this training strategy decouples the model from any specific token selection rule, allowing flexible control at inference time. At inference time, instead of random dropping, we apply task-aware token selection strategies that prioritize the most informative regions of the reference images, adapting the token budget to the input and task requirements. Extensive experiments show our method achieves a 4x increase in inference speed for multi-reference generation and an 2x for single reference generation. Importantly, this efficiency is achieved without compromising visual quality across both spatially-aligned editing and subject-driven generation.

</details>


### [283] [Lift4D: Harmonizing Single-View 3D Estimation for 4D Reconstruction In-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23688)
*Yehonathan Litman,Xiaoxuan Ma,Manan Shah,Nicolas Ugrinovic,Kris Kitani,Fernando De la Torre,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lift4D提出了一种测试时优化框架，通过因果潜在条件化的单视图3D重建模型提供时序一致的初始化，再结合遮挡感知优化与视角条件扩散先验，对可变形3D高斯泼溅表示进行"雕刻"，从而在单目视频中重建动态非刚性物体，显著优于现有方法，尤其在复杂野外场景中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么直接预测4D表示但受限于4D训练数据稀缺，要么初始化3D表示后仅依赖视频监督进行变形优化，两者均难以处理具有大形变和严重遮挡的复杂野外场景。

Method: 1) 通过因果潜在条件化适配单视图3D重建模型，生成时序一致的逐帧预测，为可变形3D高斯泼溅提供连贯初始化；2) 采用遮挡感知优化恢复可见表面细节，同时利用视角条件扩散先验补全未观测区域。

Result: Lift4D在4D重建任务上显著优于先前方法，尤其在具有严重遮挡和非刚性运动的复杂野外序列上表现突出。

Conclusion: Lift4D通过结合时序一致性初始化与遮挡感知的扩散先验优化，有效解决了单目动态非刚性物体重建中数据稀缺和大形变遮挡的挑战。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic non-rigid objects from monocular video requires integrating visual cues from direct observations with data-driven priors over geometry and appearance. Prior approaches either learn to directly predict 4D representations from visual input or initialize a 3D representation that is subsequently deformed and refined based on video evidence. However, the former are constrained by the scarcity of 4D training data, while the latter leverage priors only for the initial reconstruction and rely solely on video supervision thereafter; neither handles complex in-the-wild scenarios with large deformations and occlusions well. We present Lift4D, a test-time optimization framework that addresses both limitations. First, we adapt an existing single-view 3D reconstruction model to yield temporally consistent per-frame predictions via causal latent conditioning, providing a coherent initialization for a deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting representation. We then ``sculpt'' this representation to match the input video through an occlusion-aware optimization that faithfully recovers visible surface details while completing unobserved regions using a view-conditioned diffusion prior. We demonstrate that Lift4D clearly improves over prior 4D reconstruction methods, particularly on challenging in-the-wild sequences with severe occlusions and non-rigid motion.

</details>

### [284] [Composition: Building Community with Arts, Math, and Code (Experience Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23526)
*Isidore Mohr,Claire Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Composition 是一个关于艺术、数学和代码的免费活动系列，本文报告了其活动结构、艺术家遴选流程、征稿与推广的拓展工作，以及社区反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分享 Composition 活动系列的组织经验，为类似跨学科社区活动提供参考。

Method: 通过经验报告的形式，描述 Composition 的活动结构、艺术家选择流程、宣传推广策略以及社区响应情况。

Result: 总结了 Composition 活动的组织形式和社区反馈，但未提供具体的定量结果。

Conclusion: Composition 作为免费跨学科活动系列，其组织经验可供类似社区活动借鉴。

Abstract: Composition (https://composition.codes) is a free event series on art, mathematics, and code. This experience report covers Composition's event structure, artist selection process, outreach efforts for submissions and event promotion, and the community response.

</details>


### [285] [UI-LIC: A Unified Framework for Evaluating Learned Image Compression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23545)
*Nicholas J. Nolen,Luc Trudeau,Andrew C. Freeman*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了UI-LIC，一个用于评估和比较学习型图像压缩（LIC）系统的开源框架，集成了六个高性能LIC模型，提供统一的训练、推理和分析接口，支持与传统视频帧内编码器的比较，并配备GUI程序和交互式图像分析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIC系统的评估和比较因异构的软件栈、不同的训练条件和多样化的评估方法而变得复杂，亟需一个标准化、统一的评估框架来降低研究门槛。

Method: 构建开源软件框架UI-LIC，集成六个高性能LIC模型，提供集中控制器进行训练、推理和分析；开发GUI程序实现与传统视频帧内编码器的对比，均衡压缩码率并计算多种质量指标；提供交互式图像分析器，支持可配置的质量热图叠加显示。

Result: 实现了LIC模型的统一评估框架，降低了研究门槛，支持比较分析和主观分析，代码已开源发布。

Conclusion: UI-LIC框架有效解决了LIC系统评估的异构性问题，为研究者提供了便捷、标准化的工具，有助于推动LIC领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: The evaluation and comparison of Learned Image Compression (LIC) systems is complicated by heterogeneous software stacks, varying training conditions, and divergent evaluation methodologies. To address these challenges, we introduce UI-LIC, an open-source software framework for evaluating LIC models. We integrate six high-performance LIC models, and provide a centralized controller for performing training, inference, and analysis with shared configuration parameters. Our GUI program offers a streamlined interface to evaluate these models alongside traditional video intra-frame encoders, equalizing the compressed bitrates and calculating quality metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, VMAF, and LPIPS. Finally, we provide an interactive image analyzer with configurable quality heatmap overlays. Our framework lowers barriers to further LIC research, unlocking comparative metrics and subjective analysis with a single setup command. The open-source software is released under the MIT license and is available at github.com/BaylorMultimediaLab/UI-LIC.

</details>
